524	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
525	# cmd_runs_ls
526	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
527	
528	
529	def _summarize_run_dir(run_dir: Path) -> tuple[str, str, str]:
530	    """Return (status, last_event_kind, last_ts) for a run directory.
531	
532	    Per FLAG-P5-006: only ``aborted`` vs ``in-progress`` are reliably
533	    distinguishable in V1. A naturally completed plan still leaves
534	    ``active_run.json`` in place, so the "complete" bucket is mostly
535	    unobservable.
536	    """
537	    events_path = run_dir / "events.jsonl"
538	    if not events_path.is_file():
539	        return "in-progress", "", ""
540	    events = read_events(events_path)
541	    if not events:
542	        return "in-progress", "", ""
543	    last = events[-1]
544	    last_kind = str(last.get("kind", ""))
545	    last_ts = str(last.get("ts", ""))
546	    status = "aborted" if last_kind == "run_aborted" else "in-progress"
547	    return status, last_kind, last_ts
548	
549	
550	def cmd_runs_ls(
551	    argv: Sequence[str],
552	    *,
553	    projects_root: Optional[Path] = None,
554	) -> int:
555	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog="artagents runs ls", add_help=True)
556	    parser.add_argument("--project", default=None, help="optional project slug filter")
557	    try:
558	        args = parser.parse_args(list(argv))
559	    except SystemExit as exc:
560	        return int(exc.code or 2)
561	
562	    if args.project is not None:
563	        try:
564	            slug = validate_project_slug(args.project)
565	        except Exception as exc:
566	            _print_err(f"runs ls: {exc}")
567	            return 1
568	        project_dirs = [project_dir(slug, root=projects_root)]
569	    else:
570	        root = resolve_projects_root(projects_root)
571	        if not root.is_dir():
572	            return 0
573	        project_dirs = sorted(p for p in root.iterdir() if p.is_dir())
574	
575	    rows: list[tuple[str, str, str, str, str]] = []
576	    for proj in project_dirs:
577	        runs_root = proj / "runs"
578	        if not runs_root.is_dir():
579	            continue
580	        for run_dir in sorted(p for p in runs_root.iterdir() if p.is_dir()):
581	            status, last_kind, last_ts = _summarize_run_dir(run_dir)

1	"""Hash-chained task-run event log."""
2	
3	from __future__ import annotations
4	
5	import errno
6	import hashlib
7	import json
8	import os
9	from datetime import UTC, datetime
10	from pathlib import Path
11	from typing import Any
12	
13	ZERO_HASH = "sha256:" + "0" * 64
14	
15	
16	class EventLogError(RuntimeError):
17	    """Raised when a task event log cannot be read or written."""
18	
19	
20	def canonical_event_json(event: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
21	    payload = {key: value for key, value in event.items() if key != "hash"}
22	    return json.dumps(payload, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"), ensure_ascii=False)
23	
24	
25	def append_event(path: str | Path, event: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
26	    events_path = Path(path)
27	    ok, _last_index, error = verify_chain(events_path)
28	    if not ok:
29	        raise EventLogError(error or f"invalid event hash chain in {events_path}")
30	
31	    previous_events = read_events(events_path)
32	    prev_hash = previous_events[-1]["hash"] if previous_events else ZERO_HASH
33	
34	    stored = dict(event)
35	    stored.pop("hash", None)
36	    stored["hash"] = _event_hash(prev_hash, stored)
37	
38	    events_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
39	    created = not events_path.exists()
40	    try:
41	        with events_path.open("a", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
42	            handle.write(json.dumps(stored, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"), ensure_ascii=False))
43	            handle.write("\n")
44	            handle.flush()
45	            os.fsync(handle.fileno())
46	        if created:
47	            _fsync_dir(events_path.parent)
48	    except OSError as exc:
49	        raise EventLogError(f"failed to append event to {events_path}: {exc}") from exc
50	    return stored
51	
52	
53	def verify_chain(path: str | Path) -> tuple[bool, int, str | None]:
54	    events_path = Path(path)
55	    try:
56	        handle = events_path.open("r", encoding="utf-8")
57	    except FileNotFoundError:
58	        return True, -1, None
59	    except OSError as exc:
60	        return False, -1, f"failed to read {events_path}: {exc}"
61	
62	    prev_hash = ZERO_HASH
63	    last_index = -1
64	    with handle:
65	        for index, line in enumerate(handle):
66	            if not line.endswith("\n"):
67	                return False, index, f"event log line {index + 1} is not newline-terminated"
68	            raw = line[:-1]
69	            if not raw:
70	                return False, index, f"event log line {index + 1} is empty"
71	            try:
72	                event = json.loads(raw)
73	            except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
74	                return False, index, f"invalid JSON on event log line {index + 1}: {exc.msg}"
75	            if not isinstance(event, dict):
76	                return False, index, f"event log line {index + 1} is not an object"
77	            stored_hash = event.get("hash")
78	            if not isinstance(stored_hash, str):
79	                return False, index, f"event log line {index + 1} is missing hash"
80	            expected_hash = _event_hash(prev_hash, event)

"""Shared helpers for Phase 5 lifecycle tests (T12-T20).

Not a test module (doesn't match test_*.py glob). Provides:
- ``make_pack``: write a tiny DSL pack module under packs/<pack>/<name>.py
- ``setup_packs_and_compile``: build packs root + compile a qualified id to JSON
- ``setup_run``: build packs + projects roots, compile, and call cmd_start
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import io
import os
from contextlib import redirect_stdout
from pathlib import Path

from artagents.core.task.lifecycle import cmd_start
from artagents.orchestrate.compile import compile_to_path


def make_pack(packs_root: Path, pack: str, module_name: str, body: str) -> Path:
    pack_dir = packs_root / pack
    pack_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    module_path = pack_dir / f"{module_name}.py"
    module_path.write_text(body, encoding="utf-8")
    return module_path


def setup_packs_and_compile(
    tmp_path: Path,
    pack: str,
    module_name: str,
    body: str,
    qualified_id: str,
) -> tuple[Path, Path]:
    """Returns (packs_root, projects_root). Compiles the qualified id."""
    packs = tmp_path / "packs"
    projects = tmp_path / "projects"
    packs.mkdir()
    projects.mkdir()
    make_pack(packs, pack, module_name, body)
    compile_to_path(qualified_id, packs_root=packs)
    return packs, projects


def setup_run(
    tmp_path: Path,
    pack: str,
    module_name: str,
    body: str,
    qualified_id: str,
    *,
    run_id: str,
    project: str = "p",
    start_actor: str = "bob",
) -> tuple[Path, Path]:
    """Compile + cmd_start. Returns (packs_root, projects_root). Sets
    ARTAGENTS_ACTOR=start_actor before cmd_start so run_started.actor==start_actor.
    Caller is responsible for adjusting ARTAGENTS_ACTOR before subsequent ack calls.
    """
    packs, projects = setup_packs_and_compile(tmp_path, pack, module_name, body, qualified_id)
    os.environ["ARTAGENTS_ACTOR"] = start_actor
    with redirect_stdout(io.StringIO()):
        rc = cmd_start(
            [qualified_id, "--project", project, "--name", run_id],
            packs_root=packs,
            projects_root=projects,
        )
    if rc != 0:
        raise RuntimeError(f"setup_run: cmd_start rc={rc}")
    return packs, projects
```

1	"""Inbox surface for external completion signals (Phase 8).
2	
3	External processes drop JSON files into ``runs/<run-id>/inbox/`` to signal
4	that an attested step has completed. ``artagents next`` consumes these
5	entries before computing the next step.
6	"""
7	
8	from __future__ import annotations
9	
10	import json
11	import logging
12	from dataclasses import dataclass
13	from pathlib import Path
14	
15	INBOX_DIR_NAME = "inbox"
16	CONSUMED_DIR_NAME = ".consumed"
17	REJECTED_DIR_NAME = ".rejected"
18	
19	_VALID_DECISIONS = ("approve", "retry", "abort")
20	
21	_LOGGER = logging.getLogger("artagents.core.task.inbox")
22	
23	
24	class InboxValidationError(Exception):
25	    """Raised internally when an inbox file fails schema validation."""
26	
27	
28	@dataclass(frozen=True)
29	class InboxEntry:
30	    path: Path
31	    step_id: str
32	    decision: str
33	    evidence: tuple[str, ...]
34	    submitted_at: str
35	    submitted_by: str
36	    item_id: str | None
37	    raw: dict
38	
39	
40	def inbox_dir(run_dir: Path) -> Path:
41	    return run_dir / INBOX_DIR_NAME
42	
43	
44	def _parse_entry(file_path: Path, raw: dict) -> InboxEntry:
45	    if not isinstance(raw, dict):
46	        raise InboxValidationError("payload must be a JSON object")
47	
48	    step_id = raw.get("step_id")
49	    if not isinstance(step_id, str) or not step_id:
50	        raise InboxValidationError("missing or empty step_id")
51	
52	    decision = raw.get("decision")
53	    if decision not in _VALID_DECISIONS:
54	        raise InboxValidationError(
55	            f"decision must be one of {_VALID_DECISIONS}, got {decision!r}"
56	        )
57	
58	    submitted_at = raw.get("submitted_at")
59	    if not isinstance(submitted_at, str):
60	        raise InboxValidationError("submitted_at must be a string")
61	
62	    submitted_by = raw.get("submitted_by")
63	    if not isinstance(submitted_by, str):
64	        raise InboxValidationError("submitted_by must be a string")
65	
66	    evidence_raw = raw.get("evidence")
67	    if evidence_raw is None:
68	        evidence: tuple[str, ...] = ()
69	    else:
70	        if not isinstance(evidence_raw, dict):
71	            raise InboxValidationError("evidence must be a JSON object")
72	        evidence_values: list[str] = []
73	        for key, value in evidence_raw.items():
74	            if not isinstance(value, str) or not value:
75	                raise InboxValidationError(
76	                    f"evidence value for {key!r} must be a non-empty string"
77	                )
78	            evidence_values.append(value)
79	        evidence = tuple(evidence_values)
80	
81	    item_id_raw = raw.get("item_id")
82	    if item_id_raw is None:
83	        item_id: str | None = None
84	    elif isinstance(item_id_raw, str) and item_id_raw:
85	        item_id = item_id_raw
86	    else:
87	        raise InboxValidationError("item_id must be a non-empty string when present")
88	
89	    return InboxEntry(
90	        path=file_path,
91	        step_id=step_id,
92	        decision=decision,
93	        evidence=evidence,
94	        submitted_at=submitted_at,
95	        submitted_by=submitted_by,
96	        item_id=item_id,
97	        raw=raw,
98	    )
99	
100	
101	def scan_inbox(run_dir: Path) -> list[InboxEntry]:
102	    """Read and validate inbox entries.
103	
104	    Returns ``[]`` when the inbox directory is absent (opt-in behavior).
105	    Subdirectories and dot-prefixed names are skipped. Malformed files are
106	    logged via ``_LOGGER.warning`` and skipped — never raised.
107	    Entries are sorted by ``(submitted_at, filename)`` for deterministic ordering.
108	    """
109	    directory = inbox_dir(run_dir)
110	    if not directory.is_dir():
111	        return []
112	
113	    entries: list[InboxEntry] = []
114	    for child in directory.iterdir():
115	        if child.name.startswith("."):
116	            continue
117	        if not child.is_file():
118	            continue
119	        try:
120	            data = child.read_bytes()
121	            payload = json.loads(data)
122	            entry = _parse_entry(child, payload)
123	        except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError, InboxValidationError) as exc:
124	            _LOGGER.warning("inbox: skipping %s: %s", child.name, exc)
125	            continue
126	        entries.append(entry)
127	
128	    entries.sort(key=lambda e: (e.submitted_at, e.path.name))
129	    return entries
130	
131	
132	def pending_count(run_dir: Path) -> int:
133	    return len(scan_inbox(run_dir))
134	
135	
136	def consume_inbox_entry(
137	    run_dir: Path,
138	    entry: InboxEntry,
139	    *,
140	    slug: str,
141	    projects_root: Path,
142	) -> bool:
143	    """Validate an inbox entry against the current cursor and consume it.
144	
145	    Full implementation lands in a later task; this stub keeps the API
146	    importable for re-export and lifecycle-hook landing.
147	    """
148	    raise NotImplementedError("consume_inbox_entry is implemented in a later task")
149

  "new_string": "def _move_to(file_path: Path, dest_dir: Path) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Move ``file_path`` to ``dest_dir/<sha256>``.\n\n    The sha256-of-file-bytes filename (FLAG-P8-002) avoids any post-hoc\n    events.jsonl re-read to derive the just-appended event hash. On OSError\n    we unlink the original so a stuck file does not loop the inbox.\n    \"\"\"\n    try:\n        dest_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n        digest = hashlib.sha256(file_path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()\n        target = dest_dir / digest\n        os.replace(file_path, target)\n    except OSError as exc:\n        _LOGGER.warning(\n            \"inbox: failed to move %s to %s (%s); unlinking\", file_path.name, dest_dir.name, exc\n        )\n        try:\n            file_path.unlink(missing_ok=True)\n        except OSError:\n            pass\n\n\ndef consume_inbox_entry(\n    run_dir: Path,\n    entry: InboxEntry,\n    *,\n    slug: str,\n    projects_root: Path,\n) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"Validate ``entry`` against the current cursor and consume it.\n\n    Returns ``True`` when an event was appended (file moved to .consumed/),\n    ``False`` for stale or rejected entries. Stale-cursor entries are left\n    in inbox/ so a future call can revisit them once the cursor advances;\n    rejected entries (actor-step approve, gate failure, mismatched evidence)\n    move to .rejected/<sha256> so they do not loop.\n    \"\"\"\n    project_root = projects_root / slug\n    plan_path = project_root / \"plan.json\"\n    run_id = run_dir.name\n    events_path = run_dir / \"events.jsonl\"\n    consumed_dir = run_dir / INBOX_DIR_NAME / CONSUMED_DIR_NAME\n    rejected_dir = run_dir / INBOX_DIR_NAME / REJECTED_DIR_NAME\n\n    plan = load_plan(plan_path)\n    events = read_events(events_path)\n    peek = peek_current_step(\n        plan, events, slug, project_root=project_root, run_id=run_id\n    )\n\n    if entry.decision == \"abort\":\n        append_event(\n            events_path,\n            make_run_aborted_event(run_id, reason=f\"inbox abort by {entry.submitted_by}\"),\n        )\n        clear_active_run(slug, root=projects_root)\n        _move_to(entry.path, consumed_dir)\n        return True\n\n    # approve / retry both require the cursor to be on a matching attested step.\n    if peek.exhausted or peek.step is None or isinstance(peek.step, CodeStep):\n        _LOGGER.warning(\n            \"inbox: skipping %s: cursor not on an attested step\", entry.path.name\n        )\n        return False\n    if not isinstance(peek.step, AttestedStep):\n        _LOGGER.warning(\n            \"inbox: skipping %s: cursor not on an attested step\", entry.path.name\n        )\n        return False\n\n    cursor_step_id = peek.path_tuple[-1] if peek.path_tuple else \"\"\n    if entry.step_id != cursor_step_id:\n        _LOGGER.warning(\n            \"inbox: skipping %s: step_id %r does not match current cursor %r\",\n            entry.path.name,\n            entry.step_id,\n            cursor_step_id,\n        )\n        return False\n\n    if entry.decision == \"approve\":\n        if peek.step.ack.kind == \"actor\":\n            _LOGGER.warning(\n                \"inbox: skipping %s: ack.kind=actor not supported by inbox protocol \"\n                \"(use artagents ack ...)\",\n                entry.path.name,\n            )\n            _move_to(entry.path, rejected_dir)\n            return False\n        # ack.kind == 'agent'\n        parts: list[str] = [peek.step.command, \"--agent\", entry.submitted_by]\n        for ev in entry.evidence:\n            parts.extend([\"--evidence\", ev])\n        if entry.item_id is not None:\n            parts.extend([\"--item\", entry.item_id])\n        synthesized = \" \".join(shlex.quote(p) for p in parts)\n        try:\n            gate_command(slug, synthesized, [], root=projects_root)\n        except TaskRunGateError as exc:\n            _LOGGER.warning(\"inbox: rejecting %s: %s\", entry.path.name, exc.reason)\n            _move_to(entry.path, rejected_dir)\n            return False\n        _move_to(entry.path, consumed_dir)\n        return True\n\n    # retry\n    latest = _latest_event_for_path(events, peek.path_tuple)\n    if not isinstance(latest, dict) or latest.get(\"kind\") != \"produces_check_failed\":\n        _LOGGER.warning(\n            \"inbox: skipping %s: retry requires latest event to be produces_check_failed\",\n            entry.path.name,\n        )\n        return False\n    if peek.step.ack.kind != \"agent\":\n        _LOGGER.warning(\n            \"inbox: skipping %s: retry only supported for ack.kind=agent\",\n            entry.path.name,\n        )\n        _move_to(entry.path, rejected_dir)\n        return False\n    args = AttestedArgs(\n        agent=entry.submitted_by,\n        actor=None,\n        evidence=entry.evidence,\n        item=entry.item_id,\n    )\n    try:\n        validate_attested_identity(\n            slug=slug, step=peek.step, args=args, run_started_actor=None\n        )\n    except TaskRunGateError as exc:\n        _LOGGER.warning(\"inbox: rejecting %s: %s\", entry.path.name, exc.reason)\n        _move_to(entry.path, rejected_dir)\n        return False\n    append_event(\n        events_path,\n        make_cursor_rewind_event(peek.path_tuple, reason=\"inbox retry\"),\n    )\n    _move_to(entry.path, consumed_dir)\n    return True\n\n\ndef _latest_event_for_path(events, path_tuple: tuple[str, ...]):\n    path_str = STEP_PATH_SEP.join(path_tuple)\n    path_list = list(path_tuple)\n    for ev in reversed(events):\n        if not isinstance(ev, dict):\n            continue\n        if ev.get(\"plan_step_id\") == path_str:\n            return ev\n        if ev.get(\"plan_step_path\") == path_list:\n            return ev\n    return None"
}

No inbox/ directory — opt-in surface.\n    assert scan_inbox(run_dir) == []\n"

noqa: E402\n\nfrom artagents.core.task.events import read_events, verify_chain\nfrom artagents.core.task.lifecycle import cmd_next\n\n\n_BODY_AGENT = '''from artagents.orchestrate import orchestrator, attested\n@orchestrator(\"demo.review_agent\")\ndef main(): return [attested(\"review\", command=\"review.sh\", instructions=\"please review\", ack=\"agent\")]\n'''\n\n\ndef _drop_inbox(run_dir: Path, payload: dict) -> Path:\n    inbox = run_dir / \"inbox\"\n    inbox.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    file_path = inbox / \"approve.json\"\n    file_path.write_text(json.dumps(payload), encoding=\"utf-8\")\n    return file_path\n\n\ndef test_inbox_approve_consumed_into_step_attested(tmp_path: Path) -> None:\n    packs, projects = setup_run(\n        tmp_path, \"demo\", \"review_agent\", _BODY_AGENT, \"demo.review_agent\", run_id=\"r-inbox-1\"\n    )\n    run_dir = projects / \"p\" / \"runs\" / \"r-inbox-1\"\n    events_path = run_dir / \"events.jsonl\"\n\n    inbox_file = _drop_inbox(\n        run_dir,\n        {\n            \"step_id\": \"review\",\n            \"decision\": \"approve\",\n            \"evidence\": {\"note\": \"looks good\"},\n            \"submitted_at\": \"2026-05-01T10:00:00Z\",\n            \"submitted_by\": \"external-script\",\n        },\n    )\n\n    # Inbox file moved to inbox/.consumed/, not deleted.\n    consumed_dir = run_dir / \"inbox\" / \".consumed\"\n    assert consumed_dir.is_dir()\n    assert not inbox_file.exists()\n    consumed_files = list(consumed_dir.iterdir())\n    assert len(consumed_files) == 1\n\n    # No event written.\n    assert len(read_events(events_path)) == initial_count\n    # File quarantined to inbox/.rejected/.\n    rejected_dir = run_dir / \"inbox\" / \".rejected\"\n    assert rejected_dir.is_dir()\n    assert len(list(rejected_dir.iterdir())) == 1\n    assert not inbox_file.exists()\n    assert any(\n        \"ack.kind=actor\" in record.message for record in caplog.records\n    )\n\n\ndef test_malformed_json_skipped_and_logged(tmp_path: Path, caplog) -> None:\n    packs, projects = setup_run(\n        tmp_path, \"demo\", \"review_agent\", _BODY_AGENT, \"demo.review_agent\",\n        run_id=\"r-stale-c\",\n    )\n    run_dir = projects / \"p\" / \"runs\" / \"r-stale-c\"\n    events_path = run_dir / \"events.jsonl\"\n    initial_count = len(read_events(events_path))\n\n    inbox_file = _drop(run_dir, \"broken.json\", \"not valid json {\")\n\n    os.environ[\"ARTAGENTS_ACTOR\"] = \"bob\"\n    with caplog.at_level(logging.WARNING, logger=\"artagents.core.task.inbox\"):\n        rc = _run_next(projects)\n    assert rc == 0\n\n    assert len(read_events(events_path)) == initial_count\n    assert inbox_file.exists()\n    assert any(\"broken.json\" in record.message for record in caplog.records)\n"

        Idea:
Implement Phase 8 (Inbox surface) of the ArtAgents orchestrator V1 design per docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md section 12 (Phase 8).

LAUNCHER: every `megaplan` call = `PYENV_VERSION=3.11.11 python -m megaplan ...`.

DRIVING DISCIPLINE: After every CLI call, run `megaplan status --plan <name>` and `megaplan progress --plan <name>`. If `state == finalized` and `batches_remaining > 0`, you MUST run `megaplan execute --plan <name> --confirm-destructive --batch N` for each remaining batch sequentially. DO NOT EXIT while state != done.


PHASE 8 SCOPE (from docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md):

- `runs/<run-id>/inbox/` directory for external completion-signal protocol.
- Files dropped into the inbox by external processes (humans, scripts, other tools) signal that an attested step has completed.
- Inbox files validate into events on the next `next`/`status`/`ack` call.
- Stale or malformed files are ignored (logged but do not crash).
- Files touched: `artagents/core/task/`, lifecycle status/next handlers.

EXIT CRITERIA (from design doc):
- Inbox files validate into events.
- Stale or malformed files are ignored.

WHAT TO IMPLEMENT:

1. Define inbox file format. A simple JSON shape:
   ```json
   {
     "step_id": "...",
     "decision": "approve" | "retry" | "abort",
     "evidence": { ... step-specific artifacts ... },
     "submitted_at": "ISO 8601",
     "submitted_by": "<external-system-name>"
   }
   ```

2. New module `artagents/core/task/inbox.py`:
   - `inbox_dir(run_dir) -> Path` — returns `<run_dir>/inbox/`
   - `scan_inbox(run_dir) -> list[InboxEntry]` — reads, validates, returns parsed entries. Malformed files are logged and skipped, not raised.
   - `consume_inbox_entry(run_dir, entry) -> EventRecord` — validates the entry against the current step expectations, writes a hash-chained event, then moves the inbox file to `<run_dir>/inbox/.consumed/<hash>` (or deletes — pick one and document it).

3. Hook into lifecycle handlers:
   - `artagents next`: scans inbox before computing next step. If the inbox has a valid entry for the current attested step, consume it (record event), then advance the cursor.
   - `artagents status`: scans inbox in read-only mode and surfaces "X inbox entries pending" in output.
   - `artagents ack`: not changed (ack is the explicit verb; inbox is the implicit signal channel).

4. Tests:
   - `tests/test_inbox_scan.py`: drops 3 valid + 1 malformed file, scan returns 3 valid entries and ignores the malformed one without raising.
   - `tests/test_inbox_consume.py`: full flow — attested step waits, drop a valid inbox entry, call `next`, assert event is recorded and cursor advances.
   - `tests/test_inbox_stale.py`: drop an entry referencing a step_id that doesn't match current step, assert it's ignored (logged, not consumed).

5. `runs/<run-id>/AGENT.md` template should mention the inbox surface for human/external operators.

CONSTRAINTS:
- Stay within Phase 8 scope. Do NOT touch Phase 9 golden tests.
- Additive only. Existing tests must continue to pass.
- No new dependencies.
- Inbox is opt-in: presence of `inbox/` directory triggers scanning, absence skips it.
- Honor existing patterns: hash-chained events, gate above dispatch, file-based state.

STOP CONDITION: Phase 8 done when `pytest tests/` passes with new inbox tests + status/next surface inbox entries appropriately.

ABORT \u2014 append make_run_aborted_event(run_id, reason=f'inbox abort by {entry.submitted_by}'), call clear_active_run(slug, root=projects_root), move to .consumed/, return True. The hook is a silent no-op outside task mode.\n\nINBOX SURFACE\n- External processes (humans, scripts, other tools) signal completion of an\n  attested step by dropping a JSON file into runs/{run_id}/inbox/.\n- File shape:\n    {{\n      \"step_id\": \"<id of the current attested step>\",\n      \"decision\": \"approve\" | \"retry\" | \"abort\",\n      \"evidence\": {{ \"<key>\": \"<non-empty string>\", ... }},\n      \"submitted_at\": \"<ISO 8601 timestamp>\",\n      \"submitted_by\": \"<external system or operator name>\",\n      \"item_id\": \"<optional for_each item id>\"\n    }}\n- Consume-on-next: artagents next reads inbox/, validates each file against\n  the current cursor, and appends a step_attested / item_attested /\n  cursor_rewind / run_aborted event before computing the next step.\n- Agent attestations only — actor-ack steps must use `artagents ack` (the\n  inbox file would be quarantined to inbox/.rejected/ otherwise).\n- WARNING: `artagents next` is state-mutating when inbox/ has files.\n\"\"\""

        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/.megaplan-worktrees/phase-8-20260505/all-claude

        Idea:
Implement Phase 8 (Inbox surface) of the ArtAgents orchestrator V1 design per docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md section 12 (Phase 8).

LAUNCHER: every `megaplan` call = `PYENV_VERSION=3.11.11 python -m megaplan ...`.

DRIVING DISCIPLINE: After every CLI call, run `megaplan status --plan <name>` and `megaplan progress --plan <name>`. If `state == finalized` and `batches_remaining > 0`, you MUST run `megaplan execute --plan <name> --confirm-destructive --batch N` for each remaining batch sequentially. DO NOT EXIT while state != done.


PHASE 8 SCOPE (from docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md):

- `runs/<run-id>/inbox/` directory for external completion-signal protocol.
- Files dropped into the inbox by external processes (humans, scripts, other tools) signal that an attested step has completed.
- Inbox files validate into events on the next `next`/`status`/`ack` call.
- Stale or malformed files are ignored (logged but do not crash).
- Files touched: `artagents/core/task/`, lifecycle status/next handlers.

EXIT CRITERIA (from design doc):
- Inbox files validate into events.
- Stale or malformed files are ignored.

WHAT TO IMPLEMENT:

1. Define inbox file format. A simple JSON shape:
   ```json
   {
     "step_id": "...",
     "decision": "approve" | "retry" | "abort",
     "evidence": { ... step-specific artifacts ... },
     "submitted_at": "ISO 8601",
     "submitted_by": "<external-system-name>"
   }
   ```

2. New module `artagents/core/task/inbox.py`:
   - `inbox_dir(run_dir) -> Path` — returns `<run_dir>/inbox/`
   - `scan_inbox(run_dir) -> list[InboxEntry]` — reads, validates, returns parsed entries. Malformed files are logged and skipped, not raised.
   - `consume_inbox_entry(run_dir, entry) -> EventRecord` — validates the entry against the current step expectations, writes a hash-chained event, then moves the inbox file to `<run_dir>/inbox/.consumed/<hash>` (or deletes — pick one and document it).

3. Hook into lifecycle handlers:
   - `artagents next`: scans inbox before computing next step. If the inbox has a valid entry for the current attested step, consume it (record event), then advance the cursor.
   - `artagents status`: scans inbox in read-only mode and surfaces "X inbox entries pending" in output.
   - `artagents ack`: not changed (ack is the explicit verb; inbox is the implicit signal channel).

4. Tests:
   - `tests/test_inbox_scan.py`: drops 3 valid + 1 malformed file, scan returns 3 valid entries and ignores the malformed one without raising.
   - `tests/test_inbox_consume.py`: full flow — attested step waits, drop a valid inbox entry, call `next`, assert event is recorded and cursor advances.
   - `tests/test_inbox_stale.py`: drop an entry referencing a step_id that doesn't match current step, assert it's ignored (logged, not consumed).

5. `runs/<run-id>/AGENT.md` template should mention the inbox surface for human/external operators.

CONSTRAINTS:
- Stay within Phase 8 scope. Do NOT touch Phase 9 golden tests.
- Additive only. Existing tests must continue to pass.
- No new dependencies.
- Inbox is opt-in: presence of `inbox/` directory triggers scanning, absence skips it.
- Honor existing patterns: hash-chained events, gate above dispatch, file-based state.

STOP CONDITION: Phase 8 done when `pytest tests/` passes with new inbox tests + status/next surface inbox entries appropriately.

    "FLAG-P8-001: ack.kind='actor' files must be quarantined to .rejected/ with a clear warning, NOT left in inbox/ to zombie. The same .rejected/ path catches TaskRunGateError raised mid-consume so failed-evidence files do not loop.",

    "Hash-chain integrity: every inbox-induced event must go through append_event so verify_chain returns ok=True after consume. Do NOT bypass append_event.",
    "Re-read events and recompute peek inside cmd_next AFTER the inbox consume loop \u2014 otherwise the rest of cmd_next renders pre-consume state and prints the wrong ledger / ack template.",
    "Evidence values must all be non-empty strings (FLAG-P8-003). scan_inbox should reject (log+skip) entries where any evidence value is non-string, to prevent stringified objects flowing through gate_command's --evidence tokens.",

  "meta_commentary": "Phase 8 is glue between an external file-drop protocol and the existing kernel \u2014 keep it additive and reuse helpers. Critical sequencing: land inbox.py first (T1-T3 build on each other), then wire lifecycle hooks (T4-T6), then re-export (T7), then write tests (T8-T10), then run them (T11). FLAG resolutions to keep front-of-mind: (1) FLAG-P8-001 \u2014 branch on peek.step.ack.kind in consume_inbox_entry; actor approve files MUST move to .rejected/ to avoid zombies (test_inbox_stale.py sub-case (b) is the canary). (2) FLAG-P8-002 \u2014 use sha256(file_bytes) for the moved-file name, NOT the appended event hash (which would require post-hoc events.jsonl re-read). (3) FLAG-P8-003 \u2014 scan_inbox must reject entries whose evidence values are non-string or empty so junk does not flow through gate_command's --evidence tokens. (4) FLAG-P8-004 \u2014 InboxEntry exposes item_id; documented JSON shape includes it. (5) FLAG-P8-005 \u2014 cmd_next becomes state-mutating when inbox files exist; AGENT.md template must call this out. Byte-stability invariants: when inbox/ is absent, cmd_next and cmd_status produce identical output to pre-Phase-8 \u2014 existing test_lifecycle_next.py / test_lifecycle_status.py / test_preamble_byte_identical_across_two_calls are the canaries; if any of those break, your hook is leaking output. After consuming files in cmd_next, MUST re-read events and recompute peek so the rest of cmd_next observes post-consume state. Hash-chain integrity: every inbox-induced event flows through append_event \u2014 verify_chain ok=True is a hard requirement. Do not modify cmd_ack. No new dependencies. inbox.py should stay under ~300 lines; resist defensive wrapping around already-validated dataclass fields.",

noqa: E402\nfrom artagents.core.task.events import read_events, verify_chain  # noqa: E402\nfrom artagents.core.task.lifecycle import cmd_next  # noqa: E402\n\nBODY = '''from artagents.orchestrate import orchestrator, attested\n@orchestrator(\"demo.review_agent\")\ndef main(): return [attested(\"review\", command=\"review.sh\", instructions=\"please review\", ack=\"agent\")]\n'''\n\nwith tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:\n    tmp = Path(td)\n    packs, projects = setup_run(\n        tmp, \"demo\", \"review_agent\", BODY, \"demo.review_agent\", run_id=\"r-repro\"\n    )\n    run_dir = projects / \"p\" / \"runs\" / \"r-repro\"\n    inbox = run_dir / \"inbox\"\n    inbox.mkdir()\n    payload = {\n        \"step_id\": \"review\",\n        \"decision\": \"approve\",\n        \"evidence\": {\"note\": \"looks good\"},\n        \"submitted_at\": \"2026-05-05T12:00:00Z\",\n        \"submitted_by\": \"external-script\",\n    }\n    (inbox / \"approve.json\").write_text(json.dumps(payload), encoding=\"utf-8\")\n\n    os.environ[\"ARTAGENTS_ACTOR\"] = \"bob\"\n    with redirect_stdout(io.StringIO()), redirect_stderr(io.StringIO()):\n        rc = cmd_next([\"--project\", \"p\"], projects_root=projects)\n\n    print(f\"cmd_next rc={rc}\")\n    events_path = run_dir / \"events.jsonl\"\n    ok, last_idx, err = verify_chain(events_path)\n    print(f\"verify_chain ok={ok} last_index={last_idx}\")\n    print(\"events.jsonl tail:\")\n    for ev in read_events(events_path)[-3:]:\n        print(f\"  {ev.get('kind')}  step={ev.get('plan_step_id')}  attestor={ev.get('attestor_id')}\")\n    consumed = list((inbox / \".consumed\").iterdir()) if (inbox / \".consumed\").is_dir() else []\n    print(f\"inbox/.consumed/: {[f.name for f in consumed]}\")\n    print(f\"inbox top-level: {[f.name for f in inbox.iterdir() if f.is_file()]}\")\n\n    # follow-up call shows exhausted\n    buf = io.StringIO()\n    with redirect_stdout(buf), redirect_stderr(io.StringIO()):\n        cmd_next([\"--project\", \"p\"], projects_root=projects)\n    print(f\"follow-up cmd_next contains 'run complete': {'run complete' in buf.getvalue()}\")\n"
}
